# Commands · M · part 2

1,444 entries, mediavortex-check-baselines to migrate. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mediavortex-check-baselines**: Verify repo-local monotone-decrease invariants declared in .claude/.conformance-baselines.json. Asserts that named files have not grown beyond their baseline (line count, import count). Complements /check-conformance for in-flight migrations. ([Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex](https://github.com/Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mediavortex-check-baselines.md))
- **mediavortex-deploy-worker**: Deploy a MediaVortex WorkerService to a bare-metal Linux host. See deploy/bringup.md for host selection. ([Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex](https://github.com/Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mediavortex-deploy-worker.md))
- **mediavortex-paths**: Use when touching a file or directory path in MediaVortex Python code -- about to call `os.path.*`, compare two paths with `==`, or do filesystem I/O on a path variable. Surfaces the `Core.Path.LocalPath / Core.Path.Path` public API and the canonical-vs-local decision before the R6 hook refuses. ([Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex](https://github.com/Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mediavortex-paths.md))
- **mediavortex-smoke-transcode**: Enqueue smoke-test transcodes with deterministic worker pinning. Uses Workers.Status='Paused' to steer claims across hosts so 1080p + 4K jobs land on different workers instead of piling on one. ([Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex](https://github.com/Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mediavortex-smoke-transcode.md))
- **mediavortex-sonarr-refresh**: Rescan + search + reassign-quality-profile for Sonarr series by title substring or all monitored. Wraps Scripts/ArrRescanAndSearchSeries.py so operator can push Sonarr to reconcile disk state, re-download missing episodes, and bulk-switch quality profiles without opening the Sonarr UI. ([Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex](https://github.com/Jeremydeeallen/MediaVortex/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/mediavortex-sonarr-refresh.md))
